## GRAND PRIX LAS VEGAS 2015 (MAIN EVENT #1)

TOP 8 COVERAGE

Modern Masters Weekend was every player’s opportunity to make *Magic* history. Aaron Lewis did just that, defeating Lucas Duchow in the finals of Grand Prix Las Vegas.

True to its billing, history was made with two of the largest Grand Prix ever occurring simultaneously in Las Vegas. Event 1, to plainly keep things straight, started at a behemoth 3,687 players on Saturday. It became a returning 366 on Sunday before a Top 8 emerged, packed with the likes of Pro Tour *Born of the Gods* winner Shaun McLaren and others.

It was a weekend full of community, grandeur, and awe. While Lewis, like his counterpart Scott Markeson in the other Grand Prix Las Vegas, rose up to represent the Madison drafters as a winner, so many more in attendance around the world walked away with an experience unlike anything before it in *Magic*.

Congratulations again to Aaron Lewis, champion of one of the Grand Prix in Las Vegas on Modern Masters Weekend!

This is the tournament coverage for the first GP Vegas main event. For information on the second GP Vegas main event, head over to the [Main Event #2 coverage page](https://web.archive.org/web/20161127131700/http://magic.wizards.com/node/390441).

DAY 2 COVERAGE

Making *Magic* history continues through Modern Masters Weekend with continuing coverage of Grand Prix Las Vegas! 3,687 players started in one half of the mammoth event here, and 366 have returned Day 2 for their chance at taking one of the titles home.

Among those that rose to the top of the thousands include Connor Kirk, Chris Fennell, Andrew Lozano, Aaron Boyce, Brian Tidwell, Sammy Batarseh, Easton Henrie, Ethan Myrick, Aaron Lewis, Matthew Dickel, and Pascal Maynard, though many more such as Hall of Fame members Luis Scott-Vargas and Ben Stark follow close behind. It will be a race from start to finish as one of *Magic*’s biggest events wraps up *Magic*’s biggest weekend.

This is the tournament coverage for the first GP Vegas main event. For information on the second GP Vegas main event, head over to the [Main Event #2 coverage page](https://web.archive.org/web/20161127131700/http://magic.wizards.com/node/390441).

DAY 1 COVERAGE

The time to make *Magic* history in Las Vegas took place over 1 long weekend. 3,687 players started in one half of the mammoth Modern Masters Weekend, and 369 will return for Day 2 to share more of the amazing weekend.

Among those that rose to the top of the thousands include Connor Kirk, Chris Fennell, Andrew Lozano, Aaron Boyce, Brian Tidwell, Sammy Batarseh, Easton Henrie, Ethan Myrick, Aaron Lewis, Matthew Dickel, and Pascal Maynard, though many more such as Hall of Fame members Luis Scott-Vargas and Ben Stark follow close behind to fight for their own shot at the historic title.

This is the tournament coverage for the first GP Vegas main event. For information on the second GP Vegas main event, head over to the [Main Event #2 coverage page](https://web.archive.org/web/20161127131700/http://magic.wizards.com/node/390441).
